Results dates are NSE's own board-meeting filings, read 2026-09-29: 79 results meetings filed for the next 60 days. Companies must file at least two working days before the meeting (SEBI LODR Regulation 29), so “not announced” does not mean “no results”. Options whose results fall on or before their exit date are not listed (467 contracts refused).

Calls — you BUY the call, it pays if the stock RISES

If you take every row here (one lot each): ₹24,39,220 in total; if every stop is hit, −₹12,55,711 — more if prices gap through the stops.
These 181 rows are on 43 stocks: several strikes of one stock are one bet, not several. Most rows are in: Financial Services 41, Fast Moving Consumer Goods 22, Automobile and Auto Components 22. Rows in one sector tend to fall together. With no trend and fairly priced options, a call is expected to lose about its charges: about −₹46,974.02 across all 181 rows, one lot each. 146 of 181 are priced above the stock’s own recent movement (implied above realised), which makes it worse. They make money only if the BUY signal works, and it failed its pre-registered final test (1,205 trades, t = 0.75; panel study, 24 Sep 2026).
BAD-DAY RISK, every row together — 1 day in 20: −₹6,30,681 or worse · worst day: −₹19,20,414 · market opens 5% lower: −₹16,83,470
Today’s 181 rows (one lot each) replayed through each of the last 497 trading days, Tue, 1 Oct 2024 to Fri, 25 Sept 2026, on real prices, with every stop and target in the market. Not a forecast: these are days that already happened. Before charges.
1 day in 20: on the worst 25 of those 497 days, all the rows together lost at least ₹6,30,681 — ₹8,39,822 on average.
Worst day, Mon, 7 Apr 2025 (NIFTY −3.24%): 43 of 43 stocks fell and 179 stops were hit, 175 of them by a gap at the open — a loss of −₹19,20,414, against −₹12,19,226 if every stop had filled at its own price (before charges).
Market opens 5% lower: −₹16,83,470. Each stock opens lower by its own measured sensitivity to NIFTY (median 1.01), and a stop fills at the open, not at its own price.
Together: on those 25 worst days, 93.4% of the stocks fell at once. These rows are closer to one bet on the market than to 43 separate ones.
Most of the bad-day loss: DRREDDY 5.3%, SBIN 5.1%, DIXON 4.8%, JIOFIN 4.6%, SHRIRAMFIN 4.1%.
Demergers, bonuses and splits are read from NSE’s own filings (Tue, 29 Sept 2026), so none of them counts as a crash.
For calls these figures are the pessimistic end. Each call is repriced with Black-76 at its own implied volatility, held where it is — and in a crash that volatility jumps, which cushions a call (India VIX moved +66% on Mon, 7 Apr 2025). By how much depends on each stock’s own option volatility, which this page has no history for.
Only on the 62 stocks the desk rates BUY or ACCUMULATE — one side per stock, the side its analysis points to.

Puts — you BUY the put, it pays if the stock FALLS

CONTRACTBUY LIMITSELL · NETSTOP NOW IV / REALISEDP(TOUCH)DECAYSCORE
No puts, by design. The desk has no bearish view, and when its own history was tested, betting on falls lost money in all 24 set-ups across all three test periods (PANEL_STUDY_ROUND3). Puts come back only if a bearish signal proves itself.

Hit rate counts only trades that filled and then finished. IF RANDOM is how often the target would come before the stop if prices moved at random — each trade's break-even, before charges. Only a hit rate above it, over enough separate bets, is evidence of an edge. The touch odds the desk showed ignore the stop, so they always run higher. Futures returns are GROSS per unit; options are NET of every charge per lot. ALL has no average return (futures move about 1.5%, options about 50%), and its money is options net a lot plus futures at 20 units, gross.

HOW THIS IS SCORED. Every price this page freezes is bought on paper at the moment it is shown. Each build then checks the market, using the day’s high and low so a move between two builds is not missed. Every ambiguity counts against the record: a target and a stop in the same window is a stop; a target in the same window as the fill is not counted; a gap through the stop exits at the open; a fill is always at the limit, never better; a price from before the level was shown can never fill it. Unfilled day orders die at 15:30. Futures are held at most 20 sessions; options until expiry. Stored in the private repository under docs/ledger/, committed on every build, and copied to the Google Sheet.
WHERE THESE PRICES COME FROM. 181 priced from a live broker book, 0 from the 2026-09-28 settlement file. 181 of 181 options priced against the live futures price. Checked against NSE's own site at 2026-09-29 11:56 IST: 16 of 16 sampled contracts are the same contract on both (the same previous close), last prices within 2.7%. Broker login OK from the build server at 2026-09-29 11:54 IST — a NIFTY quote came back. F&O ban for 2026-09-29: LICHSGFIN, SAIL — left out entirely, because NSE allows only exits in a banned stock. This page is a snapshot, rebuilt on a schedule — it is not a live feed. Check the price on your broker before placing anything. Net figures are after brokerage, STT at 0.15%, exchange, stamp and GST; the spread is extra and is only known where a live book was seen.
WHAT THIS DOES NOT ESTABLISH. That any of it makes money. The paper ledger has 146 resolved trade(s) so far — far too few for a measured hit rate — and every test fixed in advance so far, on data never used to build the rule, found no edge: the desk's own ranking (final year t = 0.75), 12-1 momentum, time-series momentum, low volatility and a 10-month market trend filter (all five FAIL). The ranking is built on things that were measured, which is not the same as an edge that was proven. Size accordingly.
